# S3 form-decoding before/after provider telemetry

Every numeric providerSummary field is derived from validated rows and retained here, including zero counters. Statistics use observed values only; no missing value becomes zero. Per-attempt failure-state arrays remain in the original validated ledger; standard telemetry reports provide pressure summaries.
